## 12. Preliminary C8-P questions to answer before code changes

- Does valueObjectBridge.ts create value_object_category_links only from mapping.contextualCategoryId?
- Does the mapper currently return contextualCategoryId only for known templates or controlled mappings?
- Can Category Derivation resolved category ids be passed to the bridge without changing existing mapper behavior?
- Should category links be created once per created/reused Value Object, or once per activity_event_value_object_link?
- How should dryRun/unresolved candidates be handled? Expected answer: do not create value_object_category_links when categoryId is null.
- How should duplicate links be prevented?
- Which metadata should be stored on value_object_category_links to show source: category_derivation?
- Should C8-P start with debug route only, or add bridge-level optional parameter that is unused unless explicitly passed?

## 13. Proposed C8-P safety boundary

- Do not change normal bridge behavior by default.
- Add optional input only, probably resolvedCategoryCandidates or resolvedCategoryIds.
- If optional category candidates are absent, bridge behavior must stay identical.
- If all resolved category ids are null because dryRun=true, bridge must not create category links.
- First runtime check should repeat C8-O2 dryRun=true and confirm still no category links are created.
- Second runtime check should use dryRun=false only after duplicate/link policy is clear.

## 14. Next step

Proceed to P4.10.0-C8-P1:

- read this inventory
- decide exact bridge integration contract
- only then modify code
